## Chapter Five.

From all these discussions, the seeker of truth <note>original Latin: *veritatis Studiosus*</note> will finally perceive that it is impossible to prepare drinkable gold <term>Aurum potabile</term> <note>A legendary medicinal liquid made from gold, believed by alchemists to be a "panacea" or universal cure-all</note> outside of the process of preparing the Universal Remedy, since the very same corrosive <term>corrosive</term> <note>original: *Corrosiff*; a potent acidic solvent used to break down metals</note> is used and applied for it as is required by the Art for the composition of the Universal Tincture <term>Universal Tincture</term> <note>The final, perfected substance—often equated with the Philosopher's Stone—capable of transforming matter and healing the body</note>. Concerning this, the greatest philosophers <note>original Latin: *in genere summi Philosophi*</note> say generally that it is impossible and unbelievable to extract the quintessence <term>fifth essence</term> of gold from the sun <note>original Latin: *Quintam essentiam auri ex sole*; alchemists used "the sun" as the symbol for gold due to its perfection and brilliance</note> without a corrosive, for without a corrosive <note>original Latin: *absque corrosivo*</note>, gold cannot be mastered; just as Paracelsus <note>Theophrastus von Hohenheim (1493–1541), a pivotal figure in the history of medicine and alchemy</note> mentions more broadly in his *Archidoxes* and may well say in his book *On Long Life* <note>original Latin: *in Libro de vita longa*</note> that the corrosive is greater than the gold itself.

But how this corrosive becomes supremely sweet in such an operation of drinkable gold, without the separation of the solvent from the solution <note>original Latin: *Separation solventis à Solutio*</note>, and how the drinkable gold becomes liquid—at this point Plato bids us be silent <note>original Latin: *jubet hic Plato quiescere*; a common alchemical phrase indicating that a secret is too sacred or dangerous to be fully explained in writing</note>. For this reason, **solar medicine, or the fifth essence of the sun** <note>original Latin: *medicina solaris vel quinta Solis essentia*; "Solar medicine" often refers to gold-based remedies or those capturing the "life-force" of the sun</note>, was so strictly and earnestly sought by our **predecessors** <note>original Latin: *Prædecessoribus*</note> and held in dignity and honor; for, above all other **essences**, it is most closely related to the **first, most perfect being and the Kabbalistic number one** <note>original Latin: *primo perfectissimo esse, & unario numero Cabalistico*; referring to the Monad or divine unity, the source of all things</note>. **Hence, first philosophy was called "wisdom" by the most ancient and solemn men, because through it one ascends to the highest knowledge of incorporeal and spiritual things, in which the intellect [comprehends] the universal** <note>original Latin: *Hinc prima philosophia ab antiquissimis & gravissimis viris sapientia dicta, quod per eam ad altissimam rerum incorporearum & spiritualium cognitionem ascenditur, in qua & intellectus universas*</note>

For this reason, a learned **author** writes that potable gold encompasses two works within itself. The second is the preservation of the same through the **Astral Salt** <term>Astral Salt</term> <note>original: *Astralische Saltz*; a spiritual or "star-derived" salt believed by alchemists to be a preservative of life and a key to making medicines permanent and effective.</note>, which guards the mantle of honor, and with the

<summary>The author defines the true nature of "potable gold," warning that it cannot be created by ignorant charlatans who do not understand the philosophical "Three Primes" (Mercury, Sulfur, and Salt). # There follow 2 Responses from the
## **F. R. C.** <note>The *Fraternitas Rosae Crucis*, or the Brotherhood of the Rosy Cross, a legendary secret society of the 17th century.</note> delivered to several of their
->**Clients.** <term>Clients: In this context, "clients" refers to applicants or seekers who had petitioned the Brotherhood for knowledge or membership.</term><-

### The First.

Every person by nature desires to have great golden and silver treasures, noble stones, and wealth, and to be great and highly esteemed before the world. **God** has also created all of this so that humanity might use it, be a master over it, and thereby recognize, praise, honor, and thank Him for His **Divine goodness** and **omnipotence**. Yet, everyone wishes to seek and receive these things only during "fair-weather" days <note>original German: *bey guten Tagen*; implies a desire for ease or times of prosperity without hardship.</note> without the effort, danger, and labor where **God** has placed them. No one wishes to dig deep, search, or find, because for a long time now, the place and the way thither have remained unknown and hidden from the **common multitude** <term>Common multitude: Referred to in the original as the *Hauffen*, the uninitiated or "profane" masses who seek only material gain.</term>. For

<summary>This page introduces the first of two formal responses from the Rosicrucian Brotherhood to their petitioners, arguing that while God created material wealth for human use, the path to finding it is hidden from those unwilling to labor or recognize the Divine.</summary>
<keywords>Rosicrucians, F.R.C., spiritual wealth, divine providence, seekers, hidden knowledge, alchemy</keywords>

to reach it is difficult and laborious—indeed, dangerous—but nonetheless not impossible to attain. Since, however, **God** does not wish to keep anything hidden from His great multitude, but rather, in this final age <note>original Latin: *Seculo*; refers to the "last age" of the world in a theological sense.</note>, and before the final Judgment breaks forth, it must be revealed to the worthy—just as **Christ** Himself speaks, albeit very obscurely so that the unworthy may not understand it, when He says that nothing must remain hidden. Thus, we are driven by the **Spirit of God** to proclaim this will of the Lord in the world, just as this message has already gone out from us and been published <note>original: *publicirt*</note> in various languages. 

But it is either received poorly by the **common multitude** <term>Common multitude: The *grossen Hauffen*, a term used by the Rosicrucians to distinguish the spiritual "elite" from the unrefined, materialistic public.</term>, or indeed despised; or it is sought and desired from us without God. For they believe that one should teach them the Art (how to "cook" gold in the **alchemical** <note>original: *Alchymische*</note> manner), or that we should quickly run to meet them with great treasures—no matter how much they desire them for their splendor, pride, war, usury, gluttony, drunkenness, unchastity, or other sins—and share our secrets with them. 

Yet they should take an example from the ten virgins <note>A reference to the Parable of the Ten Virgins in Matthew 25, where five foolish virgins fail to prepare enough oil for their lamps.</note>—where the five foolish ones demanded oil from the five wise ones—and realize that it must be attained in a much different way: through each individual's own effort, through and in **God**. Where the **moonlight** <note>In alchemy, moonlight often symbolizes the *Albedo* or "white" stage—a period of reflection, purification, and feminine energy before the final "red" stage of gold.</note> dazzles my eyes so that I stumble or fall and break a leg, I simply make a **stilt** <note>The "stilt" represents a temporary, artificial support or crutch used to navigate spiritual or physical infirmity during the "Great Work."</note> and walk slowly; that is a **balsam** to my inner **salt** <note><term>Salt</term>: In the triadic system of Paracelsus (Salt, Sulfur, Mercury), Salt represents the physical body or the principle of fixity and earthiness.</note>, which heals me again. But if I should become sweating, a lovely **sweet water** comes out of my **pores**, like **milk and honey**. Then I burn my stilts into pure **ashes**; when the ash glows without smoke, the **King’s Well** <note>The "King's Well" or "Fountain" is a classic alchemical image for the universal solvent or the source of life and transformation where the "King" (gold/spirit) is reborn.</note> gives forth the **marigolds** <note>original: *Ringelblumen*; these gold-colored flowers symbolize the solar principle or the burgeoning "gold" of the spiritual work.</note> from itself. And then three fall into the well and create a darkness of the world until the moonlight breaks through again and becomes clear.

&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;The night is passed; the **sun** gives its light, the days of the **Lord** approach; the heaven becomes pure fire and ignites the whole world. All four **elements** <note>Fire, Air, Water, and Earth.</note> melt together, and a new heaven and earth is born. In these short words the **treasure of the world** is comprised, with which your desire [is fulfilled—]

<summary>This page concludes a mystical allegory where the narrator describes a process of injury and healing through "inner salt," leading to an apocalyptic vision of the elements melting to form a new world.</summary>
<keywords>Albedo, inner salt, King's Well, marigolds, four elements, spiritual rebirth, alchemy, macrocosm</keywords>

...is fulfilled. This our **Brotherhood** <note>referring to the secret society, likely the Rosicrucians, whose writings often emphasize the communal sharing of divine wisdom.</note> has also shared without any recompense <note>original: *Wieder-vergelten*; meaning the knowledge is given freely as a gift, not sold for profit.</note>. But present yourselves on **Christmas Day** at **St. Peter’s** again; within our **Order**, the **B. T.** <note>original: *B. T.*; in this context, it likely stands for *Beata Tinctura* (the Blessed Tincture) or *Benedictio Triunitatis* (the Blessing of the Trinity), representing the ultimate spiritual or alchemical reward.</note> shall also follow you.
